An RSU may be awarded upon satisfaction of such performance goals based on Performance Factors during any Performance Period as are set out in advance in the Participant’s Award Agreement. 88 9.3 Form and Timing of Settlement . Payment of earned RSUs shall be made as soon as practicable after the date(s) determined by the Committee and set forth in the Award Agreement. The Committee, in its sole discretion, may settle earned RSUs in cash, Shares, or a combination of both. The Committee may also permit a Participant to defer payment under a RSU to a date or dates after the RSU is earned provided that the terms of the RSU and any deferral satisfy the requirements of Section 409A of the Code. 9.4 Termination. Except as may be set forth in the Participant’s Award Agreement, vesting ceases on such Participant’s Termination Date (unless determined otherwise by the Committee). 9.5 Dividend Equivalents . The Committee may, in its discretion, specify in the applicable Award Agreement that any or all dividend equivalents or other distributions paid on Awards of RSUs prior to vesting or settlement, as applicable, be paid either in cash or in additional Shares and either on a current or deferred basis and that such dividend equivalents or other distributions may be reinvested in additional Shares, which may be subject to the same restrictions as such Awards. 10. PERFORMANCE AWARDS . 10.1 Performance Awards . A Performance Award is an award to an eligible Employee, Consultant, or Non-Employee Director of a cash bonus or a Performance Share bonus. Grants of Performance Awards shall be made pursuant to an Award Agreement. 10.2 Terms of Performance Awards . The Committee will determine, and each Award Agreement shall set forth, the terms of each award of Performance Award including, without limitation: (a) the amount of any cash bonus; (b) the number and type of Shares deemed subject to a Performance Share bonus; (c) the Performance Factors and Performance Period that shall determine the time and extent to which each Performance Award shall be settled; (d) the consideration to be distributed on settlement; and (e) the effect of the Participant’s Termination on each Performance Award. Prior to settlement the Committee shall determine the extent to which Performance Awards have been earned. 10.3 Value, Earning and Timing of Performance Shares . Any Performance Share bonus will have an initial value equal to the Fair Market Value of a Share on the date of grant. After the applicable Performance Period has ended, the holder of a Performance Share bonus will be entitled to receive a payout of the number of Shares earned by the Participant over the Performance Period, to be determined as a function of the extent to which the corresponding Performance Factors or other vesting provisions have been achieved. The Committee, in its sole discretion, may pay an earned Performance Share bonus in the form of cash, in Shares (which have an aggregate Fair Market Value equal to the value of the earned Performance Shares at the close of the applicable Performance Period) or in a combination thereof. Performance Share bonuses may also be settled in Restricted Stock. 10.4 Termination . Except as may be set forth in the Participant’s Award Agreement, vesting ceases on such Participant’s Termination Date (unless determined otherwise by the Committee). 11. PAYMENT FOR SHARE PURCHASES . Payment from a Participant for Shares purchased pursuant to this Plan may be made in cash or by check or, where expressly approved for the Participant by the Committee and where permitted by law (and to the extent not otherwise set forth in the applicable Award Agreement): (a) by cancellation of indebtedness of the Company to the Participant; (b) by surrender of shares of the Company held by the Participant that have a Fair Market Value on the date of surrender equal to the aggregate exercise price of the Shares as to which said Award will be exercised or settled; (c) by waiver of compensation due or accrued to the Participant for services rendered or to be rendered to the Company or a Parent, Subsidiary or Affiliate; (d) by consideration received by the Company pursuant to a broker-assisted or other form of cashless exercise program implemented by the Company in connection with the Plan; (e) by any combination of the foregoing; or (f) by any other method of payment as is permitted by applicable law. 89 12. GRANTS TO NON-EMPLOYEE DIRECTORS . 12.1 Types of Awards . Non-Employee Directors are eligible to receive any type of Award offered under this Plan except ISOs. Awards pursuant to this Section 12 may be automatically made pursuant to policy adopted by the Board, or made from time to time as determined in the discretion of the Board. 12.2 Eligibility . Awards pursuant to this Section 12 shall be granted only to Non-Employee Directors. A Non-Employee Director who is elected or re-elected as a member of the Board will be eligible to receive an Award under this Section 12. 12.3 Vesting, Exercisability and Settlement . Except as set forth in Section 20 21 , Awards shall vest, become exercisable and be settled as determined by the Board. With respect to Options and SARs, the exercise price granted to Non-Employee Directors shall not be less than the Fair Market Value of the Shares at the time that such Option or SAR is granted. 12.4 Election to receive Awards in Lieu of Cash . A Non-Employee Director may elect to receive his or her annual retainer payments and/or meeting fees from the Company in the form of cash or Awards or a combination thereof, as determined by the Committee. Such Awards shall be issued under the Plan. An election under this Section 12.4 shall be filed with the Company on the form prescribed by the Company. 13. WITHHOLDING TAXES . 13.1 Withholding Generally . Whenever Shares are to be issued in satisfaction of Awards granted under this Plan, the Company may require the Participant to remit to the Company, or to the Parent, Subsidiary or Affiliate employing the Participant, an amount sufficient to satisfy applicable U.S. federal, state, local and international withholding tax requirements or any other tax liability legally due from the Participant prior to the delivery of Shares pursuant to exercise or settlement of any Award. Whenever payments in satisfaction of Awards granted under this Plan are to be made in cash, such payment will be net of an amount sufficient to satisfy applicable U.S. federal, state, local and international withholding tax requirements or any other tax liability legally due from the Participant. The Fair Market Value of the Shares will be determined as of the date that the taxes are required to be withheld as required by applicable tax rules or as of the date determined by the Committee in its discretion, where permitted by applicable law. 13.2 Stock Withholding . The Committee, in its sole discretion and pursuant to such procedures as it may specify from time to time and to limitations of local law, may require or permit a Participant to satisfy such tax withholding obligations or rights or any other tax liability legally due from the Participant, in whole or in part by (without limitation) (i) paying cash, (ii) electing to have the Company withhold otherwise deliverable cash or Shares having a Fair Market Value up to the maximum statutory amount to be withheld unless a lesser amount of withholding is required to avoid adverse accounting treatment, or (iii) delivering to the Company already-owned Shares having a Fair Market Value up to the maximum amount to be withheld unless a lesser amount of withholding is required to avoid adverse accounting treatment. 14. TRANSFERABILITY . 14.1 Transfer Generally . Unless determined otherwise by the Committee or pursuant to Section 14.2, an Award may not be sold, pledged, assigned, hypothecated, transferred, or disposed of in any manner other than by will or by the laws of descent or distribution. If the Committee makes an Award transferable, including, without limitation, by instrument to an inter vivos or testamentary trust in which the Awards are to be passed to beneficiaries upon the death of the trustor (settlor) or by gift to a Permitted Transferee, such Award will contain such additional terms and conditions as the Committee deems appropriate. Unless determined otherwise by the Committee, all Awards shall be exercisable: (i) during the Participant’s lifetime only by (A) the Participant, or (B) the Participant’s guardian or legal representative; (ii) after the Participant’s death, by the legal representative of the Participant’s heirs or legatees; and (iii) in the case of all awards except ISOs, by a Permitted Transferee. 14.2 Award Transfer Program . Notwithstanding any contrary provision of the Plan, the Committee shall have all discretion and authority to determine and implement the terms and conditions of any Award Transfer Program instituted pursuant to this Section 14.2 and shall have the authority to amend the terms of any Award participating, or otherwise eligible to participate in, the Award Transfer Program, including (but not limited to) the authority to (i) amend (including to extend) the expiration date, post-termination exercise period and/or forfeiture conditions of any such Award, (ii) amend or remove any provisions of the Award relating to the Award holder’s continued service to the Company or its Parent, Subsidiary or Affiliate, (iii) amend the permissible payment methods with respect to the exercise or purchase of any such Award, (iv) amend the adjustments to be implemented in the event of changes in the capitalization and other similar events with respect to such Award, and (v) make such other changes to the terms of such Award as the Committee deems necessary or appropriate in its sole discretion. 90 15. PRIVILEGES OF STOCK OWNERSHIP; RESTRICTIONS ON SHARES . 15.1 Voting and Dividends . No Participant will have any of the rights of a stockholder with respect to any Shares until the Shares are issued to the Participant, except for any rights permitted by an applicable Award Agreement or any dividend equivalent rights granted in accordance with Section 6.6 or Section 9.5. After Shares are issued to the Participant, the Participant will be a stockholder and have all the rights of a stockholder with respect to such Shares, including the right to vote (in the case of Class A Shares) and receive all dividends or other distributions made or paid with respect to such Shares; provided , that if such Shares are Restricted Stock, then any new, additional or different securities the Participant may become entitled to receive with respect to such Shares by virtue of a stock dividend, stock split or any other change in the corporate or capital structure of the Company will be subject to the same restrictions as the Restricted Stock; provided , further , that the Participant will have no right to retain such stock dividends or stock distributions with respect to Shares that are repurchased at the Participant’s Purchase Price or Exercise Price, as the case may be, pursuant to Section 15.2. 15.2 Restrictions on Shares . At the discretion of the Committee, the Company may reserve to itself and/or its assignee(s) a right to repurchase (a “ Right of Repurchase ”) a portion of any or all Unvested Shares held by a Participant following such Participant’s Termination at any time within ninety (90) days after the later of the Participant’s Termination Date and the date the Participant purchases Shares under this Plan, for cash and/or cancellation of purchase money indebtedness, at the Participant’s Purchase Price or Exercise Price, as the case may be. 15.3 Restrictive Covenants . To the extent permitted by law, the Committee may impose restrictions on any Award with respect to non-competition, non-solicitation, confidentiality and other restrictive covenants, or requirements to comply with minimum share ownership requirements, as it deems necessary or appropriate in its sole discretion, which such restrictions may be set forth in any applicable Award Agreement or otherwise. 16. CERTIFICATES . All Shares or other securities whether or not certificated, delivered under this Plan will be subject to such stock transfer orders, legends and other restrictions as the Committee may deem necessary or advisable, including restrictions under any applicable U.S. federal, state or foreign securities law, or any rules, regulations and other requirements of the SEC or any stock exchange or automated quotation system upon which the Shares may be listed or quoted and any non-U.S. exchange controls or securities law restrictions to which the Shares are subject. 17. ESCROW; PLEDGE OF SHARES . To enforce any restrictions on a Participant’s Shares, the Committee may require the Participant to deposit all certificates representing Shares, together with stock powers or other instruments of transfer approved by the Committee, appropriately endorsed in blank, with the Company or an agent designated by the Company to hold in escrow until such restrictions have lapsed or terminated, and the Committee may cause a legend or legends referencing such restrictions to be placed on the certificates. Any Participant who is permitted to execute a promissory note as partial or full consideration for the purchase of Shares under this Plan will be required to pledge and deposit with the Company all or part of the Shares so purchased as collateral to secure the payment of the Participant’s obligation to the Company under the promissory note; provided , however , that the Committee may require or accept other or additional forms of collateral to secure the payment of such obligation and, in any event, the Company will have full recourse against the Participant under the promissory note notwithstanding any pledge of the Participant’s Shares or other collateral. In connection with any pledge of the Shares, the Participant will be required to execute and deliver a written pledge agreement in such form as the Committee will from time to time approve. The Shares purchased with the promissory note may be released from the pledge on a pro rata basis as the promissory note is paid. 18. REPRICING; EXCHANGE AND BUYOUT OF AWARDS. Without prior stockholder approval the Committee may (i) reprice Options or SARs (and where such repricing is a reduction in the Exercise Price of outstanding Options or SARs, the consent of the affected Participants is not required provided written notice is provided to them, notwithstanding any adverse tax consequences to them arising from the repricing), and (ii) with the consent of the respective Participants (unless not required pursuant to Section 5.9 of the Plan), pay cash or issue new Awards in exchange for the surrender and cancellation of any, or all, outstanding Awards. 19. SECURITIES LAW AND OTHER REGULATORY COMPLIANCE . An Award will not be effective unless such Award is in compliance with all applicable U.S. and foreign federal and state securities laws, rules and regulations of any governmental body, and the requirements of any stock exchange or automated quotation system upon which the Shares may then be listed or quoted, as they are in effect on the date of grant of the Award and also on the date of exercise or other issuance. Notwithstanding any other provision in this Plan, the Company will have no obligation to issue or deliver certificates for Shares under this Plan prior to: (a) obtaining any approvals from governmental agencies that the Company determines are necessary or advisable; and/or (b) completion of any registration or other qualification of such Shares under any state or federal or foreign law or ruling of any governmental body that the Company determines to be necessary or advisable. The Company will be under no obligation to register the Shares with the SEC or to effect compliance with the registration, qualification or listing requirements of any foreign or state securities laws, stock exchange or automated quotation system, and the Company will have no liability for any inability or failure to do so. 91 20. NO OBLIGATION TO EMPLOY . Nothing in this Plan or any Award granted under this Plan will confer or be deemed to confer on any Participant any right to continue in the employ of, or to continue any other relationship with, the Company or any Parent, Subsidiary or Affiliate or limit in any way the right of the Company or any Parent, Subsidiary or Affiliate to terminate Participant’s employment or other relationship at any time. 21. CORPORATE TRANSACTIONS . 21.1 Assumption or Replacement of Awards by Successor . In the event of a Corporate Transaction any or all outstanding Awards may be assumed or replaced by the successor corporation, which assumption or replacement shall be binding on all Participants. In the alternative, the successor corporation may substitute equivalent Awards or provide substantially similar consideration to Participants as was provided to stockholders (after taking into account the existing provisions of the Awards). Any such consideration shall be made promptly following such Corporate Transaction or on a specified date or dates following such Corporate Transaction; provided that the timing of such payment shall comply with Section 409A of the Code. The successor corporation may also issue, in place of outstanding Shares or Awards held by the Participant, substantially similar shares or other property subject to restrictions not materially less favorable to the Participant. In the event such successor or acquiring corporation (if any) refuses to assume, convert, replace or substitute Awards, as provided above, pursuant to a Corporate Transaction, then notwithstanding any other provision in this Plan to the contrary, such Awards shall have their vesting accelerate as to all shares subject to such Award (and any applicable right of repurchase fully lapse) immediately prior to the Corporate Transaction unless otherwise determined by the Board and then such Awards will terminate. In addition, in the event such successor or acquiring corporation (if any) refuses to assume, convert, replace or substitute Awards, as provided above, pursuant to a Corporate Transaction, the Committee will notify the Participant in writing or electronically that such Award will be exercisable for a period of time determined by the Committee in its sole discretion, and such Award will terminate upon the expiration of such period. Awards need not be treated similarly in a Corporate Transaction. Notwithstanding anything to the contrary herein, if the Intrinsic Value of an Option or SAR is equal to or less than zero, the Committee may, in its sole discretion, provide for the cancellation of such Awards without payment of any consideration therefor (for the avoidance of doubt, in the event of a Corporate Transaction, the Committee may, in its sole discretion, terminate any Option or SAR for which the Exercise Price is equal to or exceeds the per Share value of the consideration to be paid in the Corporate Transaction without payment of consideration therefor). 21.2 Assumption of Awards by the Company . The Company, from time to time, also may substitute or assume outstanding awards granted by another company, whether in connection with an acquisition of such other company or otherwise, by either; (a) granting an Award under this Plan in substitution of such other company’s award; or (b) assuming such award as if it had been granted under this Plan if the terms of such assumed award could be applied to an Award granted under this Plan. Such substitution or assumption will be permissible if the holder of the substituted or assumed award would have been eligible to be granted an Award under this Plan if the other company had applied the rules of this Plan to such grant. In the event the Company assumes an award granted by another company, the terms and conditions of such award will remain unchanged ( except that the Purchase Price or the Exercise Price, as the case may be, and the number and nature of Shares issuable upon exercise or settlement of any such Award will be adjusted appropriately pursuant to Section 424(a) of the Code). In the event the Company elects to grant a new Option in substitution rather than assuming an existing option, such new Option may be granted with a similarly adjusted Exercise Price. 21.3 Non-Employee Directors’ Awards . Notwithstanding any provision to the contrary herein, in the event of a Corporate Transaction, the vesting of all Awards granted to Non-Employee Directors shall accelerate and such Awards shall become exercisable (as applicable) in full prior to the consummation of such event at such times and on such conditions as the Committee determines. 22. ADOPTION AND STOCKHOLDER APPROVAL . This Plan shall be submitted for the approval of the Company’s stockholders, consistent with applicable laws, within twelve (12) months before or after the date this Plan is adopted by the Board. 23. TERM OF PLAN/GOVERNING LAW . Unless earlier terminated as provided herein, this Plan will become effective on the Effective Date and will terminate ten (10) years from the Effective Date. This Plan and all Awards granted hereunder shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of the State of Delaware. 24. AMENDMENT OR TERMINATION OF PLAN . 24.1 The Board may at any time terminate or amend this Plan in any respect, including, without limitation, amendment of any form of Award Agreement or instrument to be executed pursuant to this Plan; provided , however , that the Board will not, without the approval of the stockholders of the Company, amend this Plan in any manner that requires such stockholder approval; provided further , subject to Section 2.6 and Section 20 21 , that no such amendment or termination shall be made without the consent of the affected Participant, if such action would materially adversely 92 affect the rights of such Participant under any outstanding Award, except (x) to the extent any such amendment, alteration, suspension, discontinuance or termination is made to cause the Plan to comply with applicable law, stock market or exchange rules and regulations or accounting or tax rules and regulations or (y) to impose any “clawback” or recoupment provisions on any Awards (including any amounts or benefits arising from such Awards). 24.2 The Committee may waive any conditions or rights under, amend any terms of, or amend, alter, suspend, discontinue or terminate any Award theretofore granted (including by substituting another Award of the same or a different type), prospectively or retroactively, without the consent of any relevant Participant or holder or beneficiary of an Award; provided , however , that, subject to Section 2.6 and Section 20 21 , no such action shall materially adversely affect the rights of any affected Participant or holder or beneficiary under any Award theretofore granted under the Plan, except (x) to the extent any such action is made to cause the Plan or Award to comply with applicable law, stock market or exchange rules and regulations or accounting or tax rules and regulations, or (y) to impose any “clawback” or recoupment provisions on any Awards (including any amounts or benefits arising from such Awards). 25. NONEXCLUSIVITY OF THE PLAN . Neither the adoption of this Plan by the Board, the submission of this Plan to the stockholders of the Company for approval, nor any provision of this Plan will be construed as creating any limitations on the power of the Board to adopt such additional compensation arrangements as it may deem desirable, including, without limitation, the granting of stock awards and bonuses otherwise than under this Plan, and such arrangements may be either generally applicable or applicable only in specific cases. 26. INSIDER TRADING POLICY . Each Participant who receives an Award shall comply with any policy adopted by the Company from time to time covering transactions in the Company’s securities by Employees, officers and/or Directors of the Company. 27. CANCELLATION OR “CLAWBACK” OF AWARDS . The Committee may specify in an Award Agreement that a Participant’s rights, payments and benefits with respect to an Award shall be subject to reduction, cancellation, forfeiture or recoupment upon the occurrence of certain specified events, in addition to any otherwise applicable vesting or performance conditions of an Award. Any Awards granted under the Plan (including any amounts or benefits arising from such Awards) shall be subject to any clawback or recoupment arrangements, policies or procedures the Company has in place from time to time (including the Company’s Compensation Recoupment Policy and any other policy necessary to comply with applicable laws), and the Committee may, to the extent permitted by applicable law or by any applicable Company policy or arrangement, and shall, to the extent required, cancel or require reimbursement of any Awards granted to the Participant or any Shares issued or cash received upon vesting, exercise or settlement of any such Awards or sale of Shares underlying such Awards. 28. SECTION 409A OF THE CODE . With respect to Awards subject to Section 409A of the Code, the Plan is intended to comply with, or be exempt from, the requirements of Section 409A of the Code, and the provisions of the Plan and any Award Agreement shall be interpreted in a manner that satisfies the requirements of Section 409A of the Code, and the Plan shall be operated accordingly. If any provision of the Plan or any term or condition of any Award would otherwise frustrate or conflict with this intent, the provision, term or condition shall be interpreted and deemed amended so as to avoid this conflict. Notwithstanding anything in the Plan to the contrary, if the Committee considers a Participant to be a “specified employee” under Section 409A of the Code at the time of such Participant’s “separation from service” (as defined in Section 409A of the Code), and any amount hereunder is “deferred compensation” subject to Section 409A of the Code, any distribution of such amount that otherwise would be made to such Participant with respect to an Award as a result of such “separation from service” shall not be made until the date that is six months after such “separation from service,” except to the extent that earlier distribution would not result in such Participant’s incurring interest or additional tax under Section 409A of the Code. If an Award includes a “series of installment payments” (within the meaning of Section 1.409A-2(b)(2)(iii) of the Treasury Regulations), a Participant’s right to such series of installment payments shall be treated as a right to a series of separate payments and not as a right to a single payment, and if an Award includes “dividend equivalents” (within the meaning of Section 1.409A-3(e) of the Treasury Regulations), a Participant’s right to such dividend equivalents shall be treated separately from the right to other amounts under the Award. Notwithstanding the foregoing, the tax treatment of the benefits provided under the Plan or any Award Agreement is not warranted or guaranteed, and in no event shall the Company be liable for all or any portion of any taxes, penalties, interest or other expenses that may be incurred by a Participant on account of non-compliance with Section 409A of the Code. 29. MISCELLANEOUS . 29.1 Successors and Assigns; Severability . The terms of the Plan shall be binding upon and inure to the benefit of the Company and any successor entity, including any successor entity contemplated by Section 20 21 . If any provision of the Plan or any Award Agreement is or becomes or is deemed to be invalid, illegal or unenforceable in any jurisdiction, or as to any Participant or Award, or would disqualify the Plan or any Award under any law deemed applicable by the Committee, such provision shall be construed or deemed amended to conform to applicable laws, or if it cannot be so construed or deemed amended without, in the determination of the Committee, materially altering the 93 intent of the Plan or the Award Agreement, such provision shall be stricken as to such jurisdiction, Participant or Award, and the remainder of the Plan and any such Award Agreement shall remain in full force and effect. 29.2 No Trust or Fund Created . Neither the Plan nor any Award shall create or be construed to create a trust or separate fund of any kind or a fiduciary relationship between the Company and a Participant or any other person. To the extent that any person acquires a right to receive payments from the Company pursuant to an Award, such right shall be no greater than the right of any unsecured general creditor of the Company. 29.3 No Right to Awards . No Employee, Consultant, Director, Participant, or other person shall have any claim to be granted any Award under the Plan, and there is no obligation for uniformity of treatment of employees, Participants or holders or beneficiaries of Awards under the Plan. The terms and conditions of Awards need not be the same with respect to each recipient. Any Award granted under the Plan shall be a one-time Award that does not constitute a promise of future grants. The Company, in its sole discretion, maintains the right to make available future grants under the Plan. No payment pursuant to the Plan shall be taken into account in determining any benefits under any severance, pension, retirement, savings, profit sharing, group insurance, welfare or other benefit plan of the Company or any Affiliate, except to the extent otherwise expressly provided in writing in such other plan or an agreement thereunder. 30. FOUNDER ALIGNMENT AGREEMENT This Founder Alignment Agreement (this “ Agreement ”) is made as of October 20, 2026 (the “ Execution Date ”), by and between Meta Platforms, Inc., a Delaware corporation (the “ Company ”), and Mark Zuckerberg (the “ Founder ” and, together with the Company, the “ Parties ”). RECITALS WHEREAS , the Board of Directors (the “ Board ”) of the Company, acting upon the recommendation of a special committee of the Board duly appointed pursuant to Section 144 of the DGCL and comprised of disinterested directors with respect to matters set forth in this Agreement, and the Company’s stockholders have approved the adoption of an Amended and Restated Certificate of Incorporation of the Company (the “ Amended & Restated Charter ”) to authorize the creation of a non-voting class of common stock of the Company, par value $0.000006 per share, designated as “Class C Common Stock” (the “ Class C Common Stock ”), to be filed with the Secretary of State of the State of Delaware no later than two (2) business days after the Execution Date and effective January 19, 2027 (the “ Amended & Restated Charter Effective Date ”); WHEREAS , the Board has declared a dividend of one (1) newly issued share of Class C Common Stock on each outstanding share of the Company’s Class A Common Stock, par value $0.000006 per share (the “ Class A Common Stock ”), and each outstanding share of the Company’s Class B Common Stock, par value $0.000006 per share (the “ Class B Common Stock ”), as of a record date the Board will determine in good faith therefor (the “ Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend ”), with the payment of such dividend to be made no later than the fifth business day following the Amended & Restated Charter Effective Date; WHEREAS , a condition to the payment of the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend is that this Agreement continues to be a valid and binding obligation of the Parties in full force and effect as of the payment of the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend; WHEREAS , the Company expects to issue shares of Class C Common Stock from time to time to raise capital, to fund acquisitions, to compensate employees, and for other general corporate purposes, each of which will dilute the economic interest of every stockholder, including the Founder; WHEREAS , the Board has considered from time to time whether, in addition to the actions described above, it would be advisable and in the best interests of the Company to further the alignment between the interests of the Founder and the Company’s other stockholders by issuing to the Founder equity or equity-based incentive awards or other compensatory instruments of the kind granted to the Company’s other executive officers and/or of the kind granted to executive officers of certain of the Company’s peer companies, which would entail compensation expense and dilution borne by all of the Company’s stockholders; WHEREAS , previously the Board, acting through its Compensation, Nominating & Governance Committee, determined, as disclosed in the Company’s annual proxy statements, that the Founder’s historical equity ownership sufficiently aligned his interests with those of the Company’s other stockholders such that no equity or equity-based incentive awards or other compensatory instruments were warranted, and since 2013, the Founder has not received any such award or other compensatory instrument; WHEREAS , the Board has determined that the alignment between the interests of the Founder and the Company’s other stockholders is best preserved, and compensation expense and dilution of all of the Company stockholders are best avoided, through the Founder’s continued executive service at the Company and his retention of a substantial economic ownership interest in addition to his majority voting interest; WHEREAS , the Founder will receive the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend and any Additional Dividend solely in his capacity as a stockholder, pro rata with and on the same terms as every other holder of Class A Common Stock and Class B Common Stock (other than as set forth in this Agreement); and WHEREAS , in furtherance of the foregoing and as a condition to the payment of the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend, the Founder has agreed, on behalf of himself and the Holder Group, to the restrictions on the Sale of Stapled Class C Shares set forth herein, including a requirement, commencing on the payment of the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend and subject to the terms set forth herein, that the Holder Group must Sell or convert a proportionate number of shares of Class B Common Stock or Sell a proportionate number of shares of Class A Common Stock, or apply Stapling Credits, for every Sale of Stapled Class C Shares by the Holder Group as set forth herein, and the Founder has agreed to the further covenants set forth in Sections 4 , 6 and 8 . 98 AGREEMENT NOW, THEREFORE , in consideration of the foregoing and the mutual promises, agreements and covenants set forth herein, and for other good and valuable consideration the receipt and adequacy of which the Parties acknowledge, the Parties hereby agree as follows: 1. date; and (iv) such total return is annualized by raising (a) the quotient obtained by dividing (x) such value in the preceding clause (iii) by (y) the equitably adjusted Initial 30-Day Trailing Average Price to (b) a power equal to (x) 365 divided by (y) the number of calendar days elapsed in such period and (c) subtracting one (1); provided that all applicable share prices and per-share cash dividends or distributions shall be equitably adjusted to reflect the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend and any stock split, stock dividend, recapitalization, spin-off, split-off or similar event. time); provided that any Stapled Class C Share that is the subject of a Permitted Transfer to a Permitted Transferee shall remain a Stapled Class C Share following such Permitted Transfer until such Stapled Class C Share is redesignated or becomes an Unstapled Class C Share pursuant to the terms of this Agreement, and the Permitted Transferee shall be subject to all terms and conditions set forth herein until the Stapled Class C Shares held by such Permitted Transferee are redesignated or become Unstapled Class C Shares pursuant to the terms of this Agreement. “ Stapling Credit ” shall have the meaning set forth in Section 2(d)(i) . “ Transfer ” shall have the meaning set forth in the Amended & Restated Charter as if such definition applied to the shares of Class A Common Stock and Class C Common Stock as well, mutatis mutandis . “ Unstapled Class C Shares ” shall mean any shares of Class C Common Stock that are not Stapled Class C Shares. “ Unstapling Capacity ” shall mean, as of a specified time, the sum of (i) the number of shares of Class A Common Stock and Class B Common Stock then held by the Holder Group and (ii) the number of then available and unapplied Stapling Credits. “ Unstapling Ratio ” shall mean, as of immediately prior to any Sale of Stapled Class C Shares, (i) if the number of Stapled Class C Shares then held by the Holder Group is less than or equal to the Unstapling Capacity, one (1), and (ii) if the number of Stapled Class C Shares then held by the Holder Group is greater than the Unstapling Capacity, a fraction, (x) the numerator of which is the Unstapling Capacity and (y) the denominator of which is the number of Stapled Class C Shares then held by the Holder Group. 2. Sales of Class C Common Stock . 101 a. From and after the payment of the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend, the Founder shall not, and shall cause each member of the Holder Group not to, Sell any Stapled Class C Shares, unless, in connection with any Sale of Stapled Class C Shares, (i) a member of the Holder Group, no later than two (2) business days after the settlement date of such Sale, either (A) Sells a number of shares of Class A Common Stock or Class B Common Stock, or (B) converts a number of shares of Class B Common Stock into shares of Class A Common Stock, or any combination thereof, in each case of clause (A) and (B), equal in the aggregate to the product (rounded up to the nearest whole share) of the number of Stapled Class C Shares so Sold and the Unstapling Ratio, or (ii) Stapling Credits equal to the product of the number of Stapled Class C Shares so Sold and the Unstapling Ratio are applied to such Sale pursuant to Section 2(d) , it being agreed that, unless otherwise designated by the Founder, any Stapling Credits then accrued and not previously applied shall automatically be applied to any Sale at a rate of a number of Stapling Credits equal to the Unstapling Ratio per Stapled Class C Share Sold, until all such Stapling Credits have been exhausted, and the requirements of Section 2(a)(i) shall apply only to Stapled Class C Shares Sold in such Sale for which no Stapling Credit remains available. b. The Company shall not (i) instruct or permit its transfer agent to process the de-legending of any Stapled Class C Shares in violation of this Agreement or (ii) preclear or approve any 10b5-1 trading arrangement providing for any Sale of Stapled Class C Shares in violation of this Agreement. The Stapled Class C Shares held by the Holder Group will be subject to restrictive legends, stop transfer orders, or other appropriate instructions in place with the Company’s transfer agent. In furtherance of the foregoing, the Company shall cooperate promptly with the Holder Group and instruct its transfer agent as necessary to promptly remove such restrictive legends, stop transfer orders, or instructions, as applicable, with respect to any (a) Stapled Class C Shares that are Sold by any member of the Holder Group in compliance with this Agreement (including by the application of Stapling Credits pursuant to Section 2(d) ) or (b) Stapled Class C Shares that become Unstapled Class C Shares pursuant to the terms of this Agreement, and enable the Holder Group to settle such transactions promptly; provided that to the extent any Stapled Class C Shares are held in a brokerage account or by a bank or other nominee (i.e., in “street name”), the Founder agrees, and agrees to cause the Holder Group, to provide the Company with reasonably current updates regarding account statements of such holdings, so that the Company may ensure compliance hereunder, and Founder agrees to direct its broker or bank or other nominee to comply with the terms of this Agreement. c. Stapling Condition; Automatic Conversion . (i) Subject to Section 2(c)(iv) , if at any time a member of the Holder Group Sells Stapled Class C Shares in violation of Section 2(a) , then the Holder Group shall be deemed to have irrevocably converted a number of shares of Class B Common Stock owned by the Holder Group equal to the product (rounded up to the nearest whole share) of (A) the number of such Stapled Class C Shares Sold in violation of Section 2(a) and (B) the Unstapling Ratio, automatically and without any further action, into an equal number of fully paid and nonassessable shares of Class A Common Stock, with such conversion allocated among the Holder Group as designated by the Founder from time to time. Upon any such conversion of shares of Class B Common Stock to Class A Common Stock, all rights of the applicable member of the Holder Group of such shares of Class B Common Stock shall cease and the applicable member of the Holder Group in whose name or names the shares of Class A Common Stock are to be issued shall be treated for all purposes as having become the record holder or holders of such shares of Class A Common Stock. (ii) On behalf of each member of the Holder Group, the Founder authorizes the Company and its transfer agent to take any and all actions that may be necessary to cause any such conversion to occur, whether or not such member of the Holder Group has taken any action with respect thereto, including the Company providing appropriate instruction on behalf of such member of the Holder Group to its transfer agent to effect such conversion. On behalf of the Holder Group, the Founder agrees that the automatic conversion set forth in Section 2(c)(i) above shall apply to the shares of Class B Common Stock held by the Holder Group as designated by the Founder or, if the Founder fails to so designate within five (5) business days of receiving notice from the Company of the failure to comply with Section 2(a) , (1) first, to shares of Class B Common Stock owned beneficially and of record by the member of the Holder Group that is Selling Stapled Class C Shares and (2) for any remaining Stapled Class C Shares that remain unaccounted for, to shares of Class B Common Stock owned beneficially and of record by the other members of the Holder Group proportionately based on the number of shares of Class B Common Stock then held by each such member of the Holder Group, or in accordance with any other order that the Founder has designated from time to time. (iii) The Founder shall, and shall cause each member of the Holder Group to, cooperate fully with the Company in connection with any conversion pursuant to Section 2(c)(i) , and shall take all such actions as may be necessary or desirable to cause the documentation and implementation of such conversion as promptly as practicable, including delivering, or causing to be delivered, to the Company’s transfer agent such documentation as the transfer agent may reasonably require. 102 (iv) Notwithstanding Section 2(c)(i) , if any Stapled Class C Shares are Sold in violation of Section 2(a) as a result of an inadvertent error (including any error by any member of the Holder Group or any of their respective brokers, custodians or other agents, the transfer agent or the Company), no conversion of shares of Class B Common Stock shall occur under this Section 2(c) in respect of such Stapled Class C Shares, and such Sale shall be deemed for all purposes of this Agreement to have been made in compliance with Section 2(a) and shall not constitute a breach of this Agreement, if, prior to the expiration of the period ending five (5) trading days after the earlier of (1) the date on which the Company gives the Founder written notice of such Sale, which the Company shall promptly do upon being made aware of the Sale, and (2) the date on which the Founder gives the Company written notice of such Sale, which the Founder shall promptly do upon being made aware of the Sale (such period, the “ Cure Period ”), the Holder Group (A) Sells a number of shares of Class A Common Stock or Class B Common Stock, or converts a number of shares of Class B Common Stock into shares of Class A Common Stock, or any combination thereof, in the aggregate amount as would have been required in connection with a compliant Sale pursuant to Section 2(a)(i) , (B) causes such Sale to be rescinded or unwound such that the Holder Group again owns such Stapled Class C Shares, beneficially and of record, or (C) designates a number of Unstapled Class C Shares equal to the number of such Stapled Class C Shares Sold in violation of Section 2(a) as Stapled Class C Shares for all purposes of this Agreement, in which case the Stapled Class C Shares so Sold shall be deemed to have been Unstapled Class C Shares at the time of such Sale. The foregoing shall apply only if, prior to the expiration of the Cure Period, the Founder delivers to the Company written notice describing such Sale of Stapled Class C Shares and the action taken by the Holder Group pursuant to clause (A), (B) or (C) above (a “ Cure Notice ”), which Cure Notice shall also constitute the Founder’s written notice of such Sale for purposes of clause (2) above. No conversion of Class B Common Stock to Class A Common Stock pursuant to Section 2(c)(i) in respect of any Stapled Class C Shares Sold in violation of Section 2(a) shall be effective, and the Company shall not instruct the transfer agent to give effect to any such conversion, unless and until the applicable Cure Period has expired without such Sale having been cured in accordance with this Section 2(c)(iv) , in which case such conversion shall be effective as of the close of regular trading on the last trading day of such Cure Period. (d) Stapling Credits . (i) The Holder Group shall accrue one (1) credit (each, a “ Stapling Credit ”) for (A) each share of Class A Common Stock or Class B Common Stock that is Sold by any member of the Holder Group and (B) each share of Class B Common Stock that is converted by any member of the Holder Group into a share of Class A Common Stock, in each case at any time from and after the payment of the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend, whether or not any Sale of Stapled Class C Shares is then contemplated; provided that no Stapling Credit shall accrue in respect of (1) any Sale or conversion made in satisfaction of Section 2(a)(i) or Section 2(c)(iv)(A) or (2) any conversion of shares of Class B Common Stock occurring pursuant to Section 2(c)(i) . The automatic conversion of a share of Class B Common Stock into a share of Class A Common Stock upon its Sale pursuant to the Amended & Restated Charter shall give rise to one (1) Stapling Credit only. (ii) Stapling Credits shall not expire and may be applied to any Sale of Stapled Class C Shares regardless of the period of time elapsed between the Sale or conversion giving rise to the Stapling Credit and such Sale of Stapled Class C Shares. Stapling Credits may be accrued, held and applied in fractional amounts. (iii) Upon each Sale of Stapled Class C Shares by a member of the Holder Group that is not accompanied by a Sale or conversion pursuant to Section 2(a)(i) because the Founder has designated as such, Stapling Credits then accrued and not previously applied shall automatically be applied in accordance with Section 2(a)(ii) with respect to such Sale. A Sale of Stapled Class C Shares to which Stapling Credits are applied shall be deemed for all purposes of this Agreement to have been made in compliance with Section 2(a) , and no conversion of shares of Class B Common Stock shall occur under Section 2(c) in respect of such Sale. (iv) The number of unapplied Stapling Credits shall be equitably adjusted for any stock split, reverse stock split, combination, reclassification or similar event affecting the Class A Common Stock, Class B Common Stock or Class C Common Stock (other than any Additional Dividend), so that one (1) Stapling Credit continues to correspond to one (1) share of Class A Common Stock or Class B Common Stock. 3. Sales or Transfers of Class A Common Stock, Class B Common Stock and Class C Common Stock . Except as set forth in Section 4 and any applicable requirement set forth in the Amended & Restated Charter for a share of Class B Common Stock to convert to a share of Class A Common Stock (but subject to any exception to such conversion set forth in the Amended & Restated Charter), this Agreement shall not limit or restrict any member of the Holder Group’s ability to Sell or otherwise transact in any shares of Class A Common Stock, Class B Common Stock, or Class C Common Stock (other than Sales of Stapled Class C Shares). 103 4. Insider Trading Policy . The Founder shall cause each member of the Holder Group to comply with the Meta Platforms, Inc. Insider Trading Policy in effect from time to time (the “ Insider Trading Policy ”), with respect to the Class A Common Stock, the Class B Common Stock, the Class C Common Stock and all other Company securities. 5. Release from Stapling; Additional Dividends . (a) Notwithstanding anything to the contrary in this Agreement, beginning on the last day of the first full calendar quarter that occurs following the payment of the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend (e.g., if the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend is paid on January 10, 2027, the first release of Stapled Class C Shares from the Sale restrictions as set forth in this Section 5(a) shall occur on June 30, 2027) and on the last day of each calendar quarter thereafter (each, a “ Calendar Quarter ”) in each case where such Calendar Quarter is a Qualifying Release Quarter (until there has occurred for thirty-two (32) Qualifying Release Quarters) a number of Stapled Class C Shares then held by the Holder Group equal to two and one-half percent (2.5%) of the number of shares of Class C Common Stock issued to the Holder Group in the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend (as equitably adjusted for any stock split, reverse stock split, combination, reclassification or similar event affecting the Class A Common Stock, Class B Common Stock or Class C Common Stock (other than any Additional Dividend)) shall be released from the Sale restrictions set forth in Section 2 of this Agreement (allocated among the Holder Group as determined by the Founder from time to time) and, upon such release, shall be deemed to be Unstapled Class C Shares; provided that the failure of the Continued Leadership Condition shall not result in the forfeiture of, or otherwise affect the Holder Group’s ownership of, any share of Class C Common Stock, and that shares not released from the Sale restrictions set forth in Section 2 of this Agreement shall continue to be held by the applicable member(s) of the Holder Group and shall remain subject to Section 2 . It is agreed that upon the thirty-second (32nd) Qualifying Release Quarter (which need not be over consecutive Calendar Quarters), all remaining Stapled Class C Shares then held by the Holder Group (equitably adjusted as noted above) shall be released from the Sale restrictions set forth in Section 2 of this Agreement and shall be deemed to be Unstapled Class C Shares. In addition, notwithstanding Section 5(b) or anything else to the contrary in this Agreement, at the first time at which the Holder Group no longer holds any shares of Class B Common Stock (whether as a result of Sale, conversion or otherwise), (i) all Stapled Class C Shares then held by the Holder Group shall be released from the Sale restrictions set forth in Section 2 and shall be deemed to be Unstapled Class C Shares and (ii) from and after such time, no shares of Class C Common Stock held or thereafter acquired by the Holder Group shall be or be deemed to be Stapled Class C Shares for any purpose of this Agreement. (b) Notwithstanding anything to the contrary in this Agreement but subject to the procedures set forth in Section 5(e) , if either the Founder or the Company determines that an Additional Dividend Date has occurred, such Party may deliver to the Board a certificate signed by an authorized representative of the Founder or an officer of the Company, as applicable, in a form prescribed by the Company (a “ Dividend Certificate ”) certifying the occurrence of an Additional Dividend Date and setting forth in reasonable detail (i) the Additional Dividend Date, (ii) the Doubling Event that occurred in connection with such Additional Dividend Date and (iii) the IRR as of the Additional Dividend Date. The Board shall, no later than ten (10) business days following the delivery of a Dividend Certificate (or, if the Dividend Certificate has been objected to pursuant to Section 5(e) , the date on which such objection is resolved with the conclusion that an Additional Dividend Date has in fact occurred), declare, and, within a reasonable period of time following such declaration, the Company shall pay an Additional Dividend of one (1) share of Class C Common Stock (none of which shall be deemed to be Stapled Class C Shares if the Continued Leadership Condition is satisfied on the Additional Dividend Date) on each then outstanding share of Class A Common Stock, Class B Common Stock and Class C Common Stock (such dividend, an “ Additional Dividend ”), subject to Section 173 of the DGCL; provided , that if an Additional Dividend cannot be paid in compliance with Section 173 of the DGCL at such time, the Board shall, promptly following such time as it determines that the conditions set forth in Section 173 of the DGCL are satisfied, declare, and, within a reasonable period of time following such declaration, the Company shall pay such Additional Dividend. If the Continued Leadership Condition is not satisfied on the Additional Dividend Date, it is agreed that a number of shares of Class C Common Stock received by the Holder Group in respect of such Additional Dividend equal to the Post-Leadership Stapling Number shall be deemed to be Stapled Class C Shares for all purposes under this Agreement and shall be subject to Section 2 (it being agreed that the allocation of the Stapled Class C Shares and Unstapled Class C Shares of the Class C Common Stock issued in such Additional Dividend shall be as designated by the Founder or, if the Founder has not so designated within ten (10) business days following the payment of such Additional Dividend, allocated among the members of the Holder Group pro rata based on the number of shares of Class C Common Stock received by each in such Additional Dividend); provided that if, following such Additional Dividend Date, the Founder resumes service such that the Continued Leadership Condition is satisfied for a period of at least six (6) consecutive months, then on the date that marks the six (6) month anniversary of the Founder’s resumption of service to the Company, such Stapled Class C Shares shall automatically be released from the Sale restrictions set forth in Section 2 and shall be deemed Unstapled Class C Shares. If the Board fails to declare and pay any Additional Dividend within the periods set forth in this Section 5(b) at a time when payment of such Additional Dividend is permitted by Section 173 of the DGCL, then a 104 number of Stapled Class C Shares then held by the Holder Group equal to the number of shares of Class C Common Stock that the Holder Group would have received in such Additional Dividend (or, if fewer, all remaining Stapled Class C Shares) shall automatically be deemed to be Unstapled Class C Shares; provided that any such release shall not relieve the Company of its obligation to declare and pay such Additional Dividend. (c) Except as expressly provided for therein in such definitions, no adjustments shall be made to the 30-Day Trailing Average Price nor the Initial 30-Day Trailing Average Price for any issuance of equity for, among other reasons, acquisitions, stock-based compensation or general corporate purposes, including AI-infrastructure investments. (d) It is expressly agreed that (i) an Additional Dividend Date shall occur on the first date that both of the conditions thereof are satisfied, (ii) the failure of the occurrence of a Doubling Event if the IRR Threshold is met or the failure of the IRR Threshold to be met if there is a Doubling Event will not result in the forfeiture of an Additional Dividend and (iii) an Additional Dividend shall be declared and paid in accordance with the terms of this Agreement if and when both of the conditions are satisfied. By way of illustration, if the first Doubling Event occurs, but as of such date, the IRR Threshold has not been met, an Additional Dividend Date shall still occur if, when the IRR Threshold has been met, the Doubling Event condition remains satisfied. There are expressly no time conditions to the occurrence of any Additional Dividend Date other than as set forth in the definition thereof. For the avoidance of doubt, if more than one Additional Dividend Date occurs on the same date with respect to different Doubling Events, a separate Additional Dividend shall be declared and paid in respect of each such Additional Dividend Date. (e) The Party that did not deliver the Dividend Certificate may, acting in good faith, deliver to the Party delivering such Dividend Certificate a written objection to the calculation set forth in the Dividend Certificate, which objection shall set forth in reasonable detail the basis for such objection to such calculation. If a written objection is not delivered within five (5) business days following delivery of such Dividend Certificate, such Dividend Certificate shall be final and binding on the Parties. If a timely objection is delivered, the Parties shall cooperate in good faith to resolve such objection as promptly as practicable within fifteen (15) business days, and if such Parties are unable to resolve and do not otherwise mutually agree in writing to extend such resolution period, either Party may submit the matters remaining in dispute to a nationally recognized independent accounting firm or investment bank mutually agreed upon by the Founder and an Authorized Committee (or, if they are unable to so agree within five (5) business days, each of the Founder and the Authorized Committee shall select one such firm and the two firms so selected shall jointly select a third such firm) (the actual firm chosen, the “ Independent Expert ”), which shall be instructed to determine, acting as an expert and not as an arbitrator and solely on the basis of the terms of this Agreement and the written submissions of the Parties, the matters set forth in the Dividend Certificate that remain in dispute and, based solely on that determination, whether an Additional Dividend Date has occurred and, if so, the Additional Dividend Date, within twenty (20) business days following its engagement. The determination of the Independent Expert shall be final and binding on the Parties absent manifest error. The Parties will cooperate in good faith and use reasonable best efforts to execute a customary engagement agreement with the Independent Expert. The fees and expenses of the Independent Expert shall be borne by the Company. 6. Residence Condition . For eight (8) years following the Execution Date, for so long as the Continued Leadership Condition is satisfied, the Founder commits to remaining a resident of the State of California or any other jurisdiction in which the Company maintains its corporate headquarters unless there are material unforeseen circumstances that would render it prudent or advisable for him to reside elsewhere. 7. Repurchases of Common Stock . From and after the date of the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend, to the extent the Company, with the approval of the Board, determines to repurchase or buy back shares of Class A Common Stock, the Company and the Board shall in good faith, use its best efforts to contemporaneously repurchase or buy back a number of shares of Class C Common Stock at least equal to the number of shares of Class A Common Stock repurchased or bought back on an aggregate basis. 8. Company Sale and Equal Status . To the extent that any transaction is, or series of related transactions are, (a) a merger, consolidation, conversion or other business combination requiring the approval of the holders of the Company’s capital stock (whether or not the Company is the surviving entity) or acquisition of all or substantially all of the Company’s assets, (b) any tender or exchange offer by any third party to acquire a majority of the shares of Class A Common Stock, Class B Common Stock or Class C Common Stock or (c) any tender or exchange offer by the Company to acquire any shares of Class A Common Stock, Class B Common Stock or Class C Common Stock (any such transaction, a “ Company Sale or Recapitalization ”), no member of the Holder Group shall sell, transfer, exchange or convert, directly or indirectly, any shares of Class A Common Stock, Class B Common Stock or Class C Common Stock in, or in a transaction related to, such Company Sale or Recapitalization, for (x) with respect to such member’s shares of Class A Common Stock, an amount per share greater than that received in such Company Sale or Recapitalization by the holders of Class A Common Stock, or a form of consideration different from the form that holders of Class A Common Stock would receive, or may elect to receive, in such Company Sale or Recapitalization, (y) with respect to such member’s shares of Class B Common Stock, an amount per share greater than that received in such 105 Company Sale or Recapitalization by the other holders of Class B Common Stock, or a form of consideration different from the form that other holders of Class B Common Stock would receive, or may elect to receive, in such Company Sale or Recapitalization, or (z) with respect to such member’s shares of Class C Common Stock, an amount per share greater than that received in such Company Sale or Recapitalization by the other holders of Class C Common Stock, or a form of consideration different from the form that other holders of Class C Common Stock would receive, or may elect to receive, in such Company Sale or Recapitalization. 9. Administration of this Agreement . The Company shall establish, from time to time, such policies and procedures relating to the general administration of the terms of this Agreement, any Sales of Class A Common Stock, Class B Common Stock or Class C Common Stock permitted hereunder and any conversion of Class B Common Stock to Class A Common Stock contemplated hereby, as it may deem necessary or advisable, in each case in full compliance with the terms hereof, and shall deliver notice to the members of the Holder Group of the restrictions placed on their shares of Class B Common Stock and Class C Common Stock by this Agreement and by the Amended & Restated Charter in accordance with Sections 151(f) and 202(a) of the DGCL. A determination by an Authorized Committee made after at least five (5) business days’ prior written notice to the Founder specifying the basis for such determination and affording the Founder a reasonable opportunity to respond during such period, and, with respect to any conversion arising from an inadvertent error described in Section 2(c)(iv) , only after expiration of the applicable Cure Period without cure, that a conversion of Class B Common Stock to Class A Common Stock pursuant to Section 2(c) hereof has occurred shall be conclusive absent manifest error. 10. Scope of this Agreement . This Agreement shall not in any way constitute an amendment, modification, supplement or waiver of any right, preference, privilege, term or provision set forth or contained in the Amended & Restated Charter. 11. Termination . Except as set forth in this Section 11 , this Agreement may be terminated only by a written instrument that has been approved by an Authorized Committee and executed by the Founder and the Company. This Agreement and the transfer restrictions set forth herein shall be effective immediately upon execution and delivery of this Agreement by the Founder and the Company; provided that the Founder’s obligations set forth in Section 2 shall only become effective upon payment of the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend. No additional triggering event or condition precedent shall be required for the restrictions set forth in Section 2 to become operative. In addition, (a) the Founder may terminate this Agreement upon written notice to the Company if: (i) the Amended & Restated Charter is not filed with the Secretary of State of the State of Delaware within two (2) business days of the Execution Date, (ii) the Amended & Restated Charter is not effective by the Amended & Restated Charter Effective Date or any extension of the Amended & Restated Charter Effective Date agreed upon by the Parties, (iii) the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend shall not have been paid by the Company within five (5) business days following the Amended & Restated Charter Effective Date, subject to Section 173 of the DGCL (as such date may be extended by written agreement between the Parties), (iv) there occurs any material breach of this Agreement by the Company that, if capable of being cured, has not been cured within thirty (30) days following the Company’s receipt of written notice from the Founder describing such breach in reasonable detail or (v) any material commitment of the Company hereunder (or in any provision of the Amended & Restated Charter added in connection with the matters contemplated by this Agreement) ceases to be in full force and effect or is modified without the Founder’s prior written consent; and (b) upon approval by an Authorized Committee, the Company may terminate this Agreement upon written notice to the Founder (i) upon any material breach of this Agreement by the Founder that, if capable of being cured, has not been cured within thirty (30) days following the Founder’s receipt of written notice from the Company describing such breach in reasonable detail or (ii) if any material commitment of the Founder hereunder (or in any provision of the Amended & Restated Charter added in connection with the matters contemplated by this Agreement) ceases to be in full force and effect or is modified without the Company’s prior written consent. 12. Company Actions . The Company shall take all action necessary to (a) file the Amended & Restated Charter with the Secretary of State of the State of Delaware no later than two (2) business days after the Execution Date and to cause the Amended & Restated Charter to be effective on the Amended & Restated Charter Effective Date or any extension of the Amended & Restated Charter Effective Date agreed upon by the Parties and (b) declare the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend with a record date complying with Section 213(c) of the DGCL and a payment date that is within five (5) business days following the Amended & Restated Charter Effective Date. 13. Miscellaneous . (a) Successors and Assigns . Neither this Agreement nor any of the rights, interests or obligations hereunder shall be assigned or transferred (whether by operation of law or otherwise) by either Party without the prior written consent of the other Party, and any purported assignment or other transfer without such consent shall be void and unenforceable; provided , however , that the Company may assign or transfer this Agreement to a successor entity in connection with any merger, consolidation, conversion, reorganization or business combination transaction. Subject to the preceding sentence, this Agreement shall be binding upon, inure to the benefit of and be enforceable by the Parties and their respective successors and assigns. Nothing in this Agreement, express or implied, is intended to confer upon any person other than the Parties hereto or their respective successors and 106 assigns any rights, remedies, obligations, or liabilities of any nature whatsoever under or by reason of this Agreement. (b) Entire Agreement . This Agreement constitutes the full and entire understanding and agreement between the Parties with respect to the subject matter hereof. This Agreement is not an employment agreement and does not amend or supersede the Founder’s offer letter with the Company. All determinations with respect to the Founder’s compensation (if any) are and remain within the authority of the Board and its Compensation, Nominating & Governance Committee. (c) Tax Treatment . The Parties agree that the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend and any Additional Dividend are each intended to qualify as a stock dividend described in Section 305(a) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986, as amended (the “ Code ”), and shall take no contrary position on any U.S. federal or applicable state or local tax return unless required by applicable law (such tax treatment, the “ Intended Tax Treatment ”). In the event any change in applicable law, or the interpretation or application of any applicable law, or any change in circumstances, is reasonably expected to impact such Intended Tax Treatment, the Parties (in the case of the Company acting through an Authorized Committee) shall cooperate in good faith to restructure the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend and/or any Additional Dividend, including by effecting the same as a recapitalization for U.S. federal income tax purposes, to the extent reasonably necessary to place the Parties, as nearly as possible, in the same position as if the Intended Tax Treatment had been respected, while preserving their respective governance, economic and other interests under this Agreement. The Parties further intend and agree that they shall reasonably cooperate with each other in connection with any audit, examination or other proceeding by any taxing authority relating to the tax treatment of the Initial Class C Common Stock Dividend, any Additional Dividend or the restrictions set forth in this Agreement, including by providing reasonably requested information and documentation and by promptly notifying the other Party of any such audit, examination or proceeding. Except as set forth in Section 13(e) , each Party and its affiliates (including, with respect to the Founder, any other member of the Holder Group) shall each bear its own costs and expenses (including reasonable attorneys’ fees) and shall not reimburse any other Party or its affiliate (including, with respect to the Founder, any other member of the Holder Group) in connection with the foregoing. (d) Amendment and Waiver . This Agreement or any of its provisions may be waived (either generally or in the specific instance and whether prospectively or retroactively), amended, modified or supplemented only by a written instrument that has been approved by an Authorized Committee and executed by the Founder and the Company. Any failure of the Parties to comply with any obligation, covenant, agreement or condition in this Agreement may be waived (whether prospectively or retroactively) by the Party entitled to the benefit thereof only by a written instrument that has been signed by the Party granting such waiver and that, in the case of the Company, has been approved by an Authorized Committee. No delay on the part of any Party in exercising any right, power or privilege hereunder shall operate as a waiver thereof, nor shall any waiver on the part of any Party of any right, power or privilege hereunder operate as a waiver of any other right, power or privilege hereunder, nor shall any single or partial exercise of any right, power or privilege hereunder preclude any other or further exercise thereof or the exercise of any other right, power or privilege hereunder. (e) Expenses . Except as expressly set forth in Section 5(e) (with respect to the Independent Expert), each Party shall bear its own costs and expenses (including attorneys’ fees) incurred in connection with this Agreement, the Amended & Restated Charter and the transactions contemplated hereby and thereby. Nothing herein is intended to amend, modify or waive the Indemnification Agreement. With respect to matters relating to this Agreement, any Authorized Committee shall be advised by independent legal counsel and financial advisors, and all such expenses of any Authorized Committee in connection therewith shall be paid for by the Company. (f) Notices .
